# SC21 evidence: inventory of the host and shared sources

Ids (`H-nnn`) are drafts that child 26 replaces. The new files are in `skills/chantier/cards-next/`. Sources are read at the worktree's base (`git show HEAD:<path>`, head bf4e4c42); homes are read in the working tree. Built by `work/r5/gen.py` from `work/r5/h/*.hand`; round 6 then changed rows, the closing lists and the counts with `work/r6/host_rows.py`, and the sibling-card table by hand (the `.hand` files do not hold these changes).

## How the sources were walked

- Sources (24 files with rows): every "From today's" file of the specification's section 1 that no family inventory covers, the five files no family claims (`startup-core.md`, `role-core.md`, `host-core-claude-code.md`, `host-core-copilot.md`, `reload-rules.md`) and the four `skills/*/SKILL.md`. `startup-manifest.json` holds no rule sentence: it has the action table below. `topics.json` holds triggers and readings only: it has a completeness row. `titles.md` and `ticket-summary-example.md` (in section 1's table) are already rows of `cards-inventory-chief-of-staff.md` and are not inventoried twice.
- Markdown: one row per sentence (a full stop outside a code span; a sentence of more than 190 characters is cut again at a top-level semicolon), per table row and per fenced block; headings and blank lines give no row, and the nearest heading is the trigger. A `SKILL.md`'s frontmatter gives no row (kept unchanged). `behaviour` is the source sentence, cut at 240 characters. Script: `work/r5/hsplit.py`.
- `floor.md`: one row for its preamble and one per rule. `hosts/*.json`: one row per capability (its `means`, cut), and one more row per sentence of a `means` that protects the user or the repository; the files are byte-identical copies.
- Kind and home were assigned by hand for each unit, with the best-matching text of the new set beside it (`work/r5/hdump.py`, dumps in `work/r5/h/`). The generator stops when a unit is not covered exactly once, a topic, host row, file or line named as a home does not exist, a home line is empty or a heading, or a kind-a row has no role card, floor or refusal home. It does not judge that the home's words carry the rule: that was read by hand.
- Kinds: a protects the user or the repository; b inferable by a capable model; c incident or retro patch; d host trial and "unproved" bookkeeping; e state or procedure the CLI prints, or a topic or file carries; f duplicate.
- New home vocabulary: `card <file>:<line>` is a line of a file of `cards-next/` outside `topics/` (a role card, a `SKILL.md` text, `review-step.md`, or a shared file such as `concepts.md`); `floor <n>`; `refusal <command, message start>`; `topic <name>`; `host row <capability>`; `facts`; `proof file` (`docs/proofs`, child 24); `incidents file` (child 24); `inferable`. A kind-a row's home is only a role card, a `SKILL.md` text, the floor or a refusal, never a shared file or a topic.
- Role column: the home card's role for a kind-a row, the row's roles for a host row, else the readers of the source file.

## Manifest action table

Each action of `startup-manifest.json` at the base and its new home. The manifest's other keys (`common`, `role_core`, `hosts`, `entries`, `roles`, `startup_sections`, `limit`, `paths`) are replaced by `roles.yaml`, `brief` and `help`.

| action | today's readings | new home |
| --- | --- | --- |
| route | routing.md, ../../chantier/SKILL.md, session-start.md | link in a card: `routing.md` is named by `foreman.md`; the router's choice is in `skills/chantier-SKILL.md` |
| reload | startup-core.md, role-core.md, startup-manifest.json, session-start.md#5. Reload | topic reload; the role skill runs `brief` again |

| H-029 | concepts.md:55-56 | floor 1; card concepts.md:7 | 1. Never merge without the user's word. |

## Behaviour changes on purpose

- `startup-core.md`'s "retry once only on a transient network error" is dropped: a launcher failure is a stop (floor 3 and 4).
- `startup-core.md`'s report-fields sentence and "read every manifest reading before an action" are inferable: the `report` command and the brief's reading lines carry them.
- `review-step.md`'s "cd into the worktree" is inferable: the new text names the worktree.
- "Never tell unproved behaviour as fact" is kind d here (host trial bookkeeping, topic `host-configuration`), with the proof files; the Chief of Staff's card also holds it (never "as fact what the host marks unproved").
- The sibling Chief of Staff inventory classes the write-on-yes lines it carries as e; this file classes the source sentences it walks by their own protection. Child 25 reads both.

## Known gaps

- The host JSON files must stay byte-identical, so they still name `session-start.md`, `driver.md` and `foreman-lost-driver.md`, which the new set does not hold.
- `release-template.md`, `routing.md`, `hosts/claude-code.md` and `griller-skill.md` are named in text without a markdown link; the topic `cr-ship`'s "the shipped one" and `release-notes`' `release-template.md` are not linked.
- `floor.md`, the host JSON files and `review-step.md` hold no markdown link, nor does a role card but `chief-of-staff.md` (its `init` link).
- The Foreman's card says only "Stop no sync holder" of a sync failure; the topic `sync` holds the rest.
- Cards and topics above their target are listed with their sizes in `part3-proofs.md`, proof 3.
